B 常规重要

Aligos Therapeutics 宣布正在开发的用于治疗慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染的 Pevifoscorvir Sodium 的 2 期 B-SUPREME 研究已完成入组

Aligos Therapeutics Announces the Completion of Enrollment in the Phase 2 B-SUPREME Study of Pevifoscorvir Sodium Under Development for the Treatment of Chronic HBV Infection
正文要点中文精炼Aligos Therapeutics 宣布其 pevifoscorvir 钠治疗慢性乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染的 2 期 B-SUPREME 研究已在全球完成入组。该试验在国际各地招募了 245 名成年人,分为 HBeAg 阳性组 (131 名) 和 HBeAg 阴性组 (114 名)。这项随机双盲研究比较了 pevifoscorvir 钠单药治疗与富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯 48 周的情况,主要终点评估 HBV DNA 抑制。预计将在 2027 年第三季度末获得最重要的安全性和有效性数据。研究中的口服衣壳组装调节剂在早期试验中已显示出有希望的抗病毒活性。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表日期:2026-07-13。事件日期:所提供证据中未报告。研究时间线:入组已完成。顶线安全性和有效性数据预计将于2027年第三季度末发布。
地点与对象
全球范围内,研究地点未指定。参与者:131 名患有慢性 HBV 感染的 HBeAg+ 成人和 114 名 HBeAg- 成人。
事件
Aligos Therapeutics 宣布完成 pevifoscorvir 钠治疗慢性 HBV 感染的 2 期 B-SUPREME 研究的入组。这是一项公司报告的正在进行的临床试验。
规模、影响与风险
总共入组:245 名成人(131 名 HBeAg+ 和 114 名 HBeAg-)。由于这是临床试验注册更新,因此没有报告病例、死亡或暴露情况。主要终点包括 HBV DNA 抑制。没有说明风险级别。
应对、状态与不确定性
由于招生过多,第二次中期分析被放弃。预计将于 2027 年第三季度末公布主要结果。没有报告治疗、预防或公共卫生应对行动。不确定性:功效和安全性数据待定。

Aligos Therapeutics Announces the Completion of Enrollment in the Phase 2 B-SUPREME Study of Pevifoscorvir Sodium Under Development for the Treatment of Chronic HBV Infection

Fetched Original Body
Aligos Therapeutics Announces the Completion of Enrollment in the Phase 2 B-SUPREME Study of Pevifoscorvir Sodium Under Development for the Treatment of Chronic HBV Infection PRESS RELEASE GlobeNewswire Jul. 13, 2026, 08:00 AM SOUTH SAN FRANCISCO, Calif., July 13, 2026 (GLOBE NEWSWIRE) -- Aligos Therapeutics, Inc. (Nasdaq: ALGS, “Aligos”), a clinical stage biopharmaceutical company focused on developing novel therapeutics to address unmet medical needs in liver and viral diseases, today announced that it has completed enrollment of both the HBeAg+ (Part 1a) and HBeAg- (Part 2a) participants in the Phase 2 B-SUPREME study of pevifoscorvir sodium, under development for the treatment of chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection. Topline safety and efficacy data are expected to be available in late third quarter of 2027. Part 1a completed enrollment of 131 HBeAg+ participants. This represents an over enrollment prior to the planned second interim analysis; therefore, the second interim analysis for sample size re-estimation will not be performed. Part 2a also completed enrollment of 114 HBeAg- participants. “We are pleased to announce the completion of enrollment in our global Phase 2 B-SUPREME study of pevifoscorvir sodium,” said Lawrence Blatt, Ph.D., MBA, Chairman, President, and Chief Executive Officer of Aligos Therapeutics. “Achieving full enrollment across study sites worldwide represents an important milestone for our organization and reflects the dedication of patients, investigators, healthcare professionals, and the Aligos team. This accomplishment advances our mission to develop transformative therapies for chronic hepatitis B virus infection and to reduce the burden of end-stage liver disease and hepatocellular carcinoma. We are deeply grateful to all study participants and their families for their commitment to this research. We look forward to reporting topline results in the late third quarter of next year and to continuing the advancement of pevifoscorvir sodium toward potential registration.” About B-SUPREME The Phase 2 B-SUPREME study (NCT06963710) is a randomized, double-blind, active-controlled multicenter study evaluating the safety and efficacy of pevifoscorvir sodium monotherapy compared with tenofovir disoproxil fumarate in approximately 250 untreated HBeAg+ and HBeAg- adult subjects with chronic HBV infection for 48 weeks. The primary endpoint in the HBeAg+ part is HBV DNA <LLOQ (10 IU/mL, target detected [TD] or target not detected [TND]) and the primary endpoint in the HBeAg- part is HBV DNA <LLOQ (10 IU/mL, target not detected [TND]). The study will also evaluate the safety, pharmacokinetics, and other secondary and exploratory biomarkers, including reductions in HBV antigens and other markers of HBV infection. About pevifoscorvir sodium Pevifoscorvir sodium (formerly known as ALG-000184) was derived from initial IP licensed from the laboratory of Dr. Raymond Schinazi at Emory University, which was further optimized by Aligos. Pevifoscorvir sodium is a potent potential best/first-in-class oral small molecule capsid assembly modulator (CAM-E) being developed for chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection. Phase 1 studies have demonstrated after single and multiple daily doses that pevifoscorvir sodium was well-tolerated by study participants, with no safety signals observed, and demonstrated linear PK and excellent antiviral activity. In longer term Phase 1 studies, pevifoscorvir sodium 300mg QD x ≤96 weeks monotherapy has demonstrated sustained reductions in HBV DNA, RNA, HBsAg, HBeAg, and HBcrAg. Pevifoscorvir sodium has a regulatory path, as acknowledged by the FDA, EMA, and NMPA (China), for subsequent studies utilizing the chronic suppressive pathway. About Chronic HBV Infection Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is a life-threatening viral infection that primarily affects the liver with 240 million patients worldwide and approximately 1 million individuals become newly infected every year despite the availability of a prophylactic vaccine. Complications include cirrhosis, end-stage liver disease, and hepatocellular carcinoma, which collectively resulted in approximately 1.1 million deaths in 2024, according to the World Health Organization. Chronic HBV infection is the primary cause of liver cancer worldwide, and the mortality associated with HBV-related liver cancer continues to increase. About Aligos Aligos Therapeutics, Inc. (NASDAQ: ALGS) is a clinical stage biotechnology company founded with the mission to improve patient outcomes by developing best-in-class therapies for the treatment of liver and viral diseases. Aligos applies its science driven approach and deep R&D expertise to advance its purpose-built pipeline of therapeutics for high unmet medical needs such as chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, metabolic dysfunction-associated steatohepatitis (MASH), obesity, and coronaviruses. For more information, please visit www.aligos.com or follow us on LinkedIn or X. Forward-Looking Statements This press release contains forward-looking statements within the meaning of the U.S. Private Securities Litigation Reform Act of 1995. Any statements in this press release that are not historical facts may be considered “forward-looking statements,” including without limitation, statements regarding Aligos’ research and development activities, including regulatory status and the timing of announcements and updates relating to our regulatory filings and clinical trials, such as the availability of topline data from the B-SUPREME Study in late Q3 2027, and the advancement of pevifoscorvir sodium toward potential registration. Such forward looking statements are subject to substantial risks and uncertainties that could cause our development programs, future results, performance, or achievements to differ materially from those anticipated in the forward-looking statements. Such risks and uncertainties include, without limitation, risks and uncertainties inherent in the drug development process, including Aligos’ clinical-stage of development, the process of designing and conducting clinical trials, the regulatory approval processes, and other matters that could affect the sufficiency of Aligos’ capital resources to fund operations. For a further description of the risks and uncertainties that could cause actual results to differ from those anticipated in these forward-looking statements, as well as risks relating to the business of Aligos in general, see Aligos’ Quarterly Report on Form 10-Q filed with the Securities and Exchange Commission on May 7, 2026 and its future periodic reports to be filed or submitted with the Securities and Exchange Commission. Except as required by law, Aligos undertakes no obligation to update any forward-looking statements to reflect new information, events or circumstances, or to reflect the occurrence of unanticipated events. B 常规重要

研究揭示了乙型肝炎病毒引起的肝脏炎症的机制

Study reveals mechanism underlying HBV-induced liver inflammation
正文要点中文精炼重庆医科大学研究人员于2026年7月17日发表的一项研究揭示了慢性乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染与肝脏炎症之间的分子机制。研究小组发现,HBV 复制会破坏线粒体功能,导致活性氧 (ROS) 过度积累,从而通过 Nrf2 调节的细胞因子 IL-6 和 IL-8 激活 Jak2/Stat3 炎症通路。在 HBV 转基因小鼠和患者肝脏样本中进行的实验证实了这种 ROS 依赖性级联反应,而抗氧化剂治疗 (NAC) 可减少炎症。研究结果确定 ROS 驱动的 Jak2/Stat3 信号传导是预防慢性乙型肝炎患者炎症驱动的肝病进展的潜在治疗靶点。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026 年 7 月 17 日。事件日期:所提供的证据中未报告。
地点与对象
研究在中国重庆医科大学进行。在 HBV 转基因小鼠和 pAAV/HBV1.2 小鼠模型中得到验证。对慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染患者的人类肝脏样本进行了分析。
事件
发表在《基因与疾病》上的一项机制研究发现,ROS 依赖性信号级联可激活 Jak2/Stat3 通路,从而在慢性乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染中引发肝脏炎症反应。该事件被报道为一项科学发现,而不是一次疫情或公共卫生事件。
规模、影响与风险
尚未报告病例、死亡或暴露情况。该研究描述了 HBV 诱导的肝脏炎症的分子机制,并将 ROS 依赖性 Jak2/Stat3 激活确定为潜在的治疗靶点。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究是初步的、机制性的,基于转录组分析、细胞系实验和小鼠模型。需要在临床环境和人体试验中进一步验证。来源是一家著名的科学新闻媒体,总结了同行评审的研究。

Study reveals mechanism underlying HBV-induced liver inflammation

MediKnowledge eBooks Posters Podcasts Videos Newsletters Health & Personal Care Contact Meet the Team Advertise Search Become a Member Top Health Categories Coronavirus Disease COVID-19 Diet & Nutrition Artificial Intelligence Allergies Alzheimer's & Dementia Arthritis & Rheumatology Breast Cancer Breastfeeding Cold, Flu & Cough Dermatology Diabetes Eating Disorders Eye Health Gastrointestinal Health Heart Disease Lung Cancer Mental Health Parkinson's Disease Pregnancy Sleep Urology View Health A-Z ×Top Health Categories Coronavirus Disease COVID-19 Eating Disorders Diet & Nutrition Eye Health Artificial Intelligence Gastrointestinal Health Allergies Heart Disease Alzheimer's & Dementia Lung Cancer Arthritis & Rheumatology Mental Health Breast Cancer Parkinson's Disease Breastfeeding Pregnancy Cold, Flu & Cough Sleep Dermatology Urology Diabetes View Health A-Z Medical Home Life Sciences Home About News Life Sciences A-Z White Papers Lab Equipment Interviews Newsletters Webinars More... eBooks Posters Podcasts Videos Contact Meet the Team Advertise Search Become a Member White Papers MediKnowledge eBooks Posters Podcasts Videos Newsletters Health & Personal Care Contact Meet the Team Advertise Search Become a Member Webinars eBooks Posters Podcasts Videos Contact Meet the Team Advertise Search Become a Member Study reveals mechanism underlying HBV-induced liver inflammation Download PDF Copy Add News Medical on Googleas a preferred source Compuscript LtdJul 17 2026Reviewed Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major cause of global liver morbidity, leading to progressive necroinflammation that often culminates in cirrhosis and hepatocellular carcinoma. Despite the clinical significance of this chronic inflammatory state, the precise molecular mechanisms that initiate and sustain HBV-induced hepatic damage remain poorly understood. Related StoriesDual-compartment monitoring strategy improves evaluation of donor human liversAI model predicts effective immunotherapy combinations for liver cancerOkra supplements may lower cholesterol and inflammation markers in diabetesIn a recent Genes & Diseases study, researchers from Chongqing Medical University investigated the mechanisms underlying HBV-induced liver inflammation and identified a reactive oxygen species (ROS)-dependent signaling cascade that activates the Jak2/Stat3 pathway, thereby triggering hepatic inflammatory responses. Transcriptomic analyses of HBV transgenic mice and liver samples from patients with chronic HBV infection revealed that early hepatic inflammation is characterized by pronounced suppression of mitochondrial oxidative phosphorylation (OXPHOS), particularly genes associated with mitochondrial respiratory complexes I and III, leading to mitochondrial respiratory dysfunction. Functional studies using HBV-replicating hepatocyte cell lines demonstrated that persistent viral replication progressively impaired mitochondrial respiration, resulting in elevated intracellular ROS production. This metabolic disturbance drives excessive accumulation of ROS within the hepatocytes. Similar mitochondrial dysfunction and oxidative stress were observed in HBV replication mouse models during the early stages of liver inflammation, indicating that mitochondrial dysfunction and the resulting ROS accumulation represent initiating events in HBV-associated inflammatory responses. Mechanistic investigations demonstrated that ROS accumulation activated the antioxidant regulator Nrf2, which subsequently enhanced the production of the pro-inflammatory cytokines IL-6 and IL-8. These cytokines stimulated phosphorylation of Jak2 and Stat3 without altering total protein expression, leading to transcriptional activation of multiple inflammatory genes, including Saa1, S100a9, Icam1, and Socs3, involved in immune cell recruitment and hepatic inflammation. Pharmacological manipulation of ROS levels further confirmed this mechanism, as ROS scavenging with Mito-TEMPO or suppression of HBV replication reduced Nrf2 activation, cytokine production, Jak2/Stat3 signaling, and inflammatory gene expression, whereas ROS induction produced the opposite effects. This mechanism was further validated in vivo using a pAAV/HBV1.2 mouse model with persistent HBV replication, where increased oxidative stress coincided with elevated Nrf2 expression, enhanced IL-6 and IL-8 production, activation of Jak2/Stat3 signaling, and infiltration of inflammatory cells within the liver. Treatment with the antioxidant N-acetylcysteine (NAC) markedly reduced hepatic ROS accumulation, suppressed activation of the Jak2/Stat3 pathway, decreased inflammatory cytokine expression, and alleviated liver inflammation, demonstrating that oxidative stress is a critical upstream regulator of HBV-induced inflammatory responses. Collectively, this study establishes mitochondrial dysfunction-induced ROS accumulation as a pivotal trigger of HBV-associated hepatic inflammation through activation of the Nrf2-IL-6/IL-8-Jak2/Stat3 signaling axis. By linking viral replication to oxidative stress and inflammatory signaling, this study provides mechanistic insights into the early pathogenesis of chronic hepatitis B and identifies ROS-dependent Jak2/Stat3 activation as a promising therapeutic target for preventing inflammation-driven liver disease progression. Source:Compuscript LtdJournal reference:Song, R., et al. (2026). Activation of the Jak2/Stat3 pathway by ROS-dependent signaling cascades initiates hepatitis B virus-induced hepatic inflammatory responses. Genes & Diseases. 验尸官对缺乏关于乙型肝炎再激活处方的国家指导表示担忧 - The Pharmaceutical Journal

Coroner raises concerns over lack of national guidance on prescribing for hepatitis B reactivation - The Pharmaceutical Journal
正文要点中文精炼英国内伦敦北部的一名验尸官对一名 41 岁接受肾移植并患有慢性乙型肝炎的患者死亡后,对缺乏关于乙型肝炎重新激活的处方和监测的国家指导表示担忧。该患者于 2025 年 9 月 12 日因无意中止必要的医疗而死于并发症。验尸官于 2026 年 7 月 10 日发布的报告强调,该患者的药物已于 2025 年初停止,导致乙型肝炎重新激活。验尸官向英国病毒性肝炎小组和英国健康安全局提出了担忧,指出缺乏乙型肝炎服务的专门委托。英国肝脏研究协会 (BASL) 回应称,它并不主导临床指南的制定,而且 NHS 信托机构之间的责任也有所不同。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该事件发生于2025年9月12日,验尸官的报告于2026年7月10日发布,回复于2026年7月6日发送。
地点与对象
该活动在英国内伦敦北部举行,涉及一名接受肾移植并患有慢性乙型肝炎的 41 岁患者。
事件
一名因无意中止必要医疗而死于并发症的患者死亡后,一名验尸官对缺乏关于乙型肝炎重新激活的处方和监测的国家指导表示担忧。
规模、影响与风险
该患者死于乙型肝炎再激活的并发症。验尸官指出,乙型肝炎服务缺乏国家指导和专门委托,这可能会给其他患者带来风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
验尸官向英国病毒性肝炎小组和英国健康安全局表达了担忧。英国肝脏研究协会 (BASL) 回应称,它并不主导临床指南的制定,而且 NHS 信托机构之间的监测和处方责任也有所不同。

Coroner raises concerns over lack of national guidance on prescribing for hepatitis B reactivation - The Pharmaceutical Journal

Fetched Original Body
Coroner raises concerns over lack of national guidance on prescribing for hepatitis B reactivation A patient died from complications from reactivation of hepatitis B, after the supply of viral reactivation prophylaxis was inadvertently discontinued. Patient safety 16 July 2026 By Sophie Willis Advertisement Copy link to page Download PDF The coroner wrote that although a “large population” of patients have been diagnosed with hepatitis B through opt-out screening in emergency departments, specialised commissioning is lacking to ensure engagement with services Olga Yastremska / Alamy Stock Photo A coroner has raised concerns over a lack of national guidance on prescribing and monitoring for hepatitis B reactivation, following the death of a patient. In a ‘ Prevention of future deaths’ report ‘, published online on 10 July 2026, Richard Brittain, assistant coroner for Inner London North, stated that patient Tung Tran, aged 41 years, died from complications from reactivation of hepatitis B, “which arose from inadvertent discontinuation of necessary medical treatment”. Tran had a renal transplant in 2013 and was diagnosed with chronic hepatitis B during the process, the coroner wrote. The patient was later discharged from hepatology and continued to receive viral reactivation prophylaxis entecavir from renal transplant services. “Supply of medication changed to home delivery in early 2025 but the entecavir was inadvertently discontinued, as there was a presumption that hepatology would continue to prescribe this. Mr Tran appears to have understood this to have been an intentional change of his medication,” the coroner said. “He attended his local hospital in August 2025 with signs of acute liver disease from reactivation of hepatitis B and was transferred to the Royal Free Hospital. He was too unwell to be considered for liver transplant and sadly died on 12 September 2025.” Directing a concern over the risk of future deaths to the British Viral Hepatitis Group at the British Association for the Study of the Liver (BASL), the coroner noted: “There is a lack of national guidance regarding which services should be responsible for the monitoring and prescribing in relation to hepatitis B reactivation prevention.” He also directed a concern to the UK Health Security Agency that there is a “large population” of patients who have been found to have hepatitis B through opt-out screening in emergency departments, “but there is a lack of specialised commissioning to maintain subsequent engagement with services”. “I heard that this differs from the position with regards to hepatitis C and HIV services, even though these patient populations are smaller,” the coroner added. In its response to the report , sent on 6 July 2026, the BASL said: [The BASL] does not lead the development or publication of clinical guidelines.” Patients with chronic hepatitis B are “usually managed in specialist hepatology services and the responsibility of monitoring and prescribing lies with these services”, it added. The BASL continued that for patients at risk of reactivation of hepatitis B, guidance on antiviral therapy is provided by “the relevant specialist societies and also in local NHS trust guidance”. “There is variation between NHS trusts as to whether this monitoring and prescribing is delivered by the treating team or whether specialist hepatology services are involved,” it said. Read more: Hepatitis B — prevention, diagnosis and management Last updated 16 July 2026 14:33 Citation The Pharmaceutical Journal , PJ July 2026, Vol 320, No 8011;320(8011)::DOI:10.1211/PJ.2026.1.419732 Please leave a comment Cancel reply You must be logged in to post a comment. 乙型肝炎疫苗对几内亚比绍至关重要:伦理学家 - Medscape

Hepatitis B Vaccination Crucial for Guinea-Bissau: Ethicist - Medscape
正文要点中文精炼纽约大学医学伦理学家谴责了一项在几内亚比绍拟议的不道德研究,该研究旨在使用安慰剂对照测试 15,000 名儿童的乙型肝炎疫苗接种效果。亚瑟·L·卡普兰博士认为,该疫苗已被证明是安全有效的,并且该研究设计利用了世界上最贫穷国家之一的贫困情况。该研究已暂停,但并未最终取消,伦理学家呼吁立即实施疫苗接种计划。该评论强调了美国和全球范围内关于乙型肝炎疫苗接种政策的持续争论。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表日期:2026-07-17。事件日期:所提供证据中未报告。评论讨论了一项拟议研究及其暂停,但未提供具体事件日期。
地点与对象
几内亚比绍,西非低收入国家。受影响人群:新生儿和儿童。该评论还在政策讨论中提到了美国。
事件
一位医学伦理学家(Arthur L. Caplan 博士)公开批评了几内亚比绍一项拟议的不道德研究,该研究将在 15,000 名儿童中使用安慰剂对照来测试乙型肝炎疫苗接种的效果。该研究被暂停,但并未最终取消。伦理学家认为乙型肝炎疫苗接种至关重要,并且已被证明是安全有效的。
规模、影响与风险
所提供的证据中未报告。该评论没有提供几内亚比绍的病例数、死亡数或当前疾病负担数据。拟议的研究涉及 15,000 名儿童,但没有详细说明结果或风险。
应对、状态与不确定性
拟议的研究已暂停,但并未最终取消。疾病预防控制中心因批评而暂停了这项研究。伦理学家和其他人主张立即在几内亚比绍实施疫苗接种计划,而不是进一步研究。除了暂停之外,没有任何官方预防、检测或治疗行动的报道。

Hepatitis B Vaccination Crucial for Guinea-Bissau: Ethicist - Medscape

Caplan, PhD, has disclosed the following relevant financial relationships:Serve(d) as a director, officer, partner, employee, advisor, consultant, or trustee for: Alnylam Pharmaceuticals; Sumitomo Pharma; Otsuka Pharmaceutical; Sanofi; United Therapeutics; Grünenthal; Janssen BiotechReceived income in an amount equal to or greater than $250 from: iECURE; Alnylam Pharmaceuticals; United Therapeutics; Sumitomo Pharma; Otsuka Pharmaceutical; Sanofi July 17, 20262Add to Email AlertsHepatitis B Vaccination Works and Is Essential for GuineaBissau Says Ethicist00:0000:0004:13GO LIVEFacebookTwitter/XEmailEmbedSpeedNormalAutoplayundefined Speed Normal Autoplay 000:0004:13 LIVE This transcript has been edited for clarity. Hi. I’m Art Caplan, at the section for Medical Ethics, at NYU Grossman School of Medicine. Here's something that we don't see often in healthcare and in hospitals: hepatitis B in babies. The reason we don't is we've had decades of very effective vaccination, particularly getting a first shot to babies at birth to prevent hepatitis B, which causes liver damage and ultimately death if you get it. You can get hepatitis B through fluid and blood contact. It doesn't happen often to a baby, but it happens. For years, the World Health Organization, the American Association of Pediatrics, the Infectious Diseases Society of America, and anybody you can think of who's an expert in immunization has said that getting a vaccine to a baby to prevent hepatitis B is a good thing. SUGGESTED FOR YOUThe group that hasn't agreed with that is Robert F. Kennedy, Jr, and his antivaccine cabal at the Department of Health and Human Services. They have a view that hepatitis B vaccines are dangerous and that we give kids too many shots. Well, the idea of giving kids too many shots is ludicrous. You can't get too many shots if they work and prevent horrible diseases. This whole idea of vaccinating too much is really built around the safety argument. Kennedy decided to work with his advisory committee on immunization and get rid of the recommendation from the CDC to give babies the hepatitis B vaccine. That decision was terrible. Many people protested it. In order to bolster the idea that the hepatitis B vaccine isn't safe, he then came up with a horrible idea, reminiscent of the Tuskegee study from the 1970s. Believe it or not, Kennedy proposed to do something similar in a very poor country, Guinea-Bissau, which is one of the poorest countries not only in Africa but in the world. Guinea-Bissau is rife with hepatitis B. One in five babies are born with it.The study he proposed was to take 15,000 kids, give half of them the vaccine at birth and not give it as a control to the other half, then look for efficacy and look for safety. This is about as unethical as you could ever get. We already know the vaccine works. There is no need to determine its efficacy. It's been proven over and over. We also know for sure that it is safe. In a country like Guinea-Bissau, it’s much riskier to get hepatitis B and die of that than anything that might be associated with a vaccine.To put it bluntly, Kennedy issued a no-bid contract and said to do this randomized trial with a placebo control in a poor country exploiting their poverty. By the way, the country has been begging for the hepatitis B vaccine and to get them the money so they could vaccinate everybody. They know that it works. He said that's the place to do this study. I can't imagine a more unethical study being proposed, and I can't even imagine any journal ever publishing the results of such a study because it is so grossly unethical. For now, the study has been suspended, but it could come back. There's no agreement yet from CDC that it won't try again. The CDC just heard some of the critics like me and said, we'll put it on pause. The study should never come back. Hepatitis B vaccination works. It's safe. It is clearly contributing to low hepatitis B rates in this country. It's absolutely essential that it get into places like Guinea-Bissau. The best use of money is not some horrible, unethical study in Guinea-Bissau. It's to take that money and support getting vaccines to babies in that country. I’m Art Caplan. I'm at the Division of Medical Ethics at the NYU Grossman School of Medicine. Thank you for watching.0Comments (2)Commenting is limited to medical professionals. To comment please Log-in. B 常规重要

卫生局监测23,000名乙肝患者 - 澳门邮报

Health Bureau monitors 23,000 hepatitis B patients - The Macau Post Daily
正文要点中文精炼澳门卫生局(SSM)7月13日报告称,约有23,000名患有慢性乙型肝炎的居民正在接受医学监测。该局强调,估计约有34,000名居民受到感染,成人HBsAg阳性率为7.6%,将澳门列为中度流行地区。自 2025 年 2 月起,SSM 将免费 HBsAg 检测范围扩大到没有先前记录的 50 岁以上居民,并自 2026 年 6 月起扩大到 40 岁以上居民。到 2025 年底,约有 15,000 名居民参与了筛查。定于 7 月 25 日在 Rotunda de Carlos da Maia 举行免费匿名快速检测活动,以提高认识并鼓励筛查。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026-07-14。事件日期:2026-07-13(新闻发布会)。时间范围:自2025年2月起扩大筛查。2026年7月25日为快速检测活动。
地点与对象
澳门(中国澳门特别行政区),包括 40 岁及以上未曾进行 HBsAg 检测的居民,以及 50 岁或以上无检测记录的个人。社区协会和公共卫生机构参与其中。
事件
澳门卫生局 (SSM) 报告称,正在对约 23,000 名接受医疗管理的慢性乙型肝炎患者进行监测。该事件已得到确认,涉及公共卫生指导和筛查举措。
规模、影响与风险
约23,000名慢性乙型肝炎患者处于监测中。据2016年数据,澳门估计有34,000名感染者。成人(18岁及以上)HBsAg阳性率为7.6%(中等流行程度)。5岁以下儿童阳性率为0.03%(符合世卫组织≤0.1%的标准)。1990年代出生居民中乙型肝炎阳性率约为2%。未报告急性暴发或激增。
应对、状态与不确定性
SSM 将 HBsAg 检测范围扩大到 40 岁以上未经事先检测的居民(自 2026 年 6 月起),以及之前的 50 岁以上居民(自 2025 年 2 月起)。截至 2025 年底,约有 15,000 名居民参与了筛查。即将于 2026 年 7 月 25 日在 Rotunda de Carlos da Maia 进行免费匿名快速 HBsAg 检测。2026年澳门健康调查初步结果预计于2026年底出炉。没有证据表明具体的风险级别。

Health Bureau monitors 23,000 hepatitis B patients - The Macau Post Daily

Fetched Original Body
Free anonymous rapid tests slated for July 25 The Health Bureau (SSM) said during a press conference yesterday that about 23,000 patients with chronic hepatitis B are currently under medical monitoring and management. The press conference about the prevention and treatment of hepatitis was held at the bureau’s Public Health Emergency Personnel Residence Hall on Guia Hill yesterday. News SSM Tap Seac Health Centre Director Ng Sio Fan pointed out that starting in February of last year Health Centres’ internal system automatically added the HBsAg test* for individuals aged 50 or above who lacked a testing record. Since last month, this initiative has expanded to include individuals aged 40 and above who have never undergone the test. As of the end of last year, about 15,000 residents participated in the screening scheme. SSM Centre for Disease Control and Prevention senior official Chang Fong Nei noted that according to the data from the 2016 Macao Health Survey and the Statistics and Census Bureau’s 2016 Mid-term Population Census, the HBsAg positivity rate among local residents aged 18 or above was 7.6 percent, identifying Macau as a moderate prevalence region, with an estimated 34,000 infected local residents in the community. The age structure of Hepatitis B infections in Macau is predominantly composed of individuals over the age of 50, accounting for about 70 percent of all positive cases, she said. The latest once-in-a-decade Macao Health Survey has been conducted this year, and preliminary results are expected to be announced by the end of the year. Chang pointed out that the positivity rate among children under the age of 5 was 0.03 percent in Macau last year, meeting the World Health Organisation (WHO) international standard of a paediatric positivity rate of less or equal 0.1 percent. Chang said that vaccination has yielded significant epidemic prevention results since Macau introduced the Hepatitis B vaccine in the 1980s and incorporated it into the routine vaccination programme in the early 1990s. Consequently, the Hepatitis B positivity rate among residents born in the 1990s has dropped to about 2 percent. Public awareness & free screening campaigns Meanwhile, the bureau, in collaboration with local community associations, will jointly host “Healthy Community Flash Mob Stations” on Saturday from 2:00 p.m. to 6:00 p.m. across the peninsula, Taipa and Coloane, featuring five designated activity routes, with the aim of correcting knowledge regarding the prevention and treatment of Hepatitis B through interactive games, and participants who complete the games can receive “small gifts”, Chang added. Additionally, the “2026 World Hepatitis Day” Theme Event slated for July 25, from 2:00 p.m. to 5:00 p.m., at the public Activities Centre on Rotunda de Carlos da Maia, colloquially known as “Three Lamps” (三盞燈), will feature interactive booths and anonymous rapid Hepatitis B surface antigen testing services free of charge, aiming to assist residents in checking their infection status and to elevate public focus on Hepatitis B prevention and control. Infectious Diseases World Hepatitis Day, however, takes place annually on July 28. * An HBsAg test (Hepatitis B surface Antigen test) is a blood test used to determine if a person is currently infected with the Hepatitis B virus (HBV). The test looks for Hepatitis B surface antigens, which are proteins present on the outer surface of the virus. It is the primary and most common screening tool for Hepatitis B. – Gemini Health Bureau (SSM) Tap Seac Health Centre Director Ng Sio Fan (centre), SSM Centre for Disease Control and Prevention senior official Chang Fong Nei (right) and Sum Ip Pio from the public Conde de S. Januário Hospital Centre (CHCSJ) address the press conference about the prevention and treatment of hepatitis at the bureau’s Public Health Emergency Personnel Residence Hall on Guia Hill yesterday. – Photo: Ida Cheong Health

世卫组织加强土库曼斯坦医疗专业人员应对乙型病毒性肝炎的能力 - Turkmenportal.com

WHO strengthens medical professionals’ capacity in Turkmenistan on viral hepatitis B - Turkmenportal.com
正文要点中文精炼世界卫生组织驻土库曼斯坦国家办事处于2025年3月12日至13日举办了“根据世界卫生组织建议和国家方案诊断和治疗乙型病毒性肝炎”培训课程。该课程由世界卫生组织/欧洲顾问Alexander Nersesov教授主持,旨在提高土库曼斯坦医务人员抗击病毒性肝炎的能力。培训在传染病治疗和预防中心以视频会议方式进行,涵盖了基本知识有关乙型肝炎及其诊断、分类和治疗的信息,包括针对慢性病例、妊娠和特殊患者群体以及丁型肝炎的具体指导。参与者包括医生、初级卫生保健提供者、传染病专家以及以 Myrat Karryev 命名的国立医科大学的代表。该举措预计将改善患者的治疗效果并加强土库曼斯坦控制和管理病毒性肝炎的努力。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该事件发生于2025年3月12日至13日,该文章发表于2025年3月18日。
地点与对象
培训在土库曼斯坦举行,特别是在传染病治疗预防中心。参与者包括医生、初级卫生保健提供者、传染病专家以及以迈拉特·卡里耶夫命名的国立医科大学的代表。
事件
世界卫生组织(WHO)驻土库曼斯坦国家办事处举办了“根据世界卫生组织建议和国家方案诊断和治疗乙型病毒性肝炎”的培训课程。该课程由世界卫生组织/欧洲顾问 Alexander Nersesov 教授主持,涵盖了乙型肝炎及其诊断、分类和治疗的基本信息。
规模、影响与风险
此次培训旨在提高土库曼斯坦医疗专业人员抗击乙型病毒性肝炎的能力。该举措预计将改善患者的治疗效果,并加强土库曼斯坦控制和管理病毒性肝炎的努力。没有报告具体病例数、死亡或暴露情况。
应对、状态与不确定性
培训以视频会议模式进行,涵盖乙型病毒性肝炎诊断和治疗的最新知识和最佳实践,符合世卫组织指南和国家方案。该培训是为医疗专业人员提供有效管理病毒性肝炎所需技能的持续努力的一部分。

WHO strengthens medical professionals’ capacity in Turkmenistan on viral hepatitis B - Turkmenportal.com

Fetched Original Body
Culture Economics Education In the world Sport Tourism Technologies Health Energy Society Transport WC-2026 Business Politics Culture Economics Education In the world Sport Tourism Technologies Health Energy Society Transport WC-2026 Business Politics Culture Economics Education In the world Sport Tourism Technologies Health All categories Main / News / Health 18.03.2025 13341 Share on social network WHO strengthens medical professionals’ capacity in Turkmenistan on viral hepatitis B The World Health Organization (WHO) Country Office in Turkmenistan held a training course on the "Diagnostics and treatment of viral hepatitis B according to WHO recommendations and national protocol" from March 12 to 13, 2025. The course led by WHO/Europe consultant, Professor Alexander Nersesov, aims to enhance the capacity of Turkmen medical professionals in their effort to combat viral hepatitis B. The skill-building session was conducted at the Infectious disease treatment-prevention center in videoconference mode. The participants of the event were doctors specializing in the diagnosis and treatment of viral hepatitis from the Infectious disease treatment-prevention center of the Directorate of Infectious Diseases Centers, primary health care providers, infectious diseases specialists from the institutions at the district level, and representatives from the department of infectious diseases at the State Medical University named after Myrat Karryev. The training covered essential information on hepatitis B, its diagnosis, classification, and treatment, with specific guidance for chronic cases, pregnancy, and special patient groups, as well as hepatitis D. This training is designed to equip medical professionals with the updated knowledge and best practices in viral hepatitis B diagnosis and treatment, aligning with both WHO guidelines and national protocols. By enhancing the skills of healthcare providers, this initiative will contribute to improved patient outcomes and strengthen Turkmenistan's efforts to control and manage viral hepatitis. 卫生局跟进23,000名乙肝患者 - TDM

Health Bureau Follows Up 23,000 Hepatitis B Patients - TDM
正文要点中文精炼据 2026 年 7 月 14 日报道,澳门卫生局正在通过免费筛查计划对约 23,000 名乙型肝炎检测呈阳性的居民进行跟踪。卫生局指出,50 岁及以上的居民中有 6.8% 的乙肝检测呈阳性,免费筛查的资格已扩大到 40 岁的人群。此次跟踪是持续公共卫生监测的一部分,预计将在 2026 年第四季度公布十年一次的健康调查结果。2026 年。没有提供爆发或传播的详细信息。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发表时间:2026年07月14日 21:51(澳门时间)。事件日期:所提供证据中未报告。筛查数据反映截至发表当日的当前监测状态。
地点与对象
澳门(中国特别行政区)。人群:符合免费乙肝筛查条件的40岁及以上居民。所提供的证据中未报告特定社区或设施。
事件
澳门卫生局正在对通过免费筛查计划检测出乙型肝炎呈阳性的约23,000名居民进行随访。该局报告称,约6.8%的50岁及以上居民检测呈阳性,免费筛查的资格已扩大到40岁的居民。这是一项持续的公共卫生监测和跟踪活动,而不是疫情调查。
规模、影响与风险
筛查人群:40岁以上居民(具体数字未报告)。阳性病例:50岁以上居民中的阳性率为6.8%(具体数字未报告)。随访队列:大约 23,000 名乙型肝炎患者。所提供的证据中没有报告死亡、暴露或传播动态。风险:所提供的证据中未明确说明。
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生局正在将免费乙型肝炎筛查的资格扩大到 40 岁的居民。十年一次的健康调查结果将于 2026 年第四季度公布。所提供的证据中没有详细说明具体的调查、检测、隔离、接触者追踪、预防或治疗行动。未解决:筛查个体的确切数量、阳性病例总数和后续方案。来源限制:无(全部内容已审核)。
